# Village Improvements Ledger

Baseline before Amendment 1: 46 av-* entities + earlier works.
Categories: D=detail node · E=entity · L=light · M=motion/comp ·
S=socket/seat · F=fix · G=greenery node · P=path stone node

## Cycle 0 (baseline, pre-amendment) — 528 counted, summarized
(These are the already-standing works; recounted here as the starting ledger.
Breakdown: 4 houses ≈ 163 D + 4 E; hall 60 D + 1 E + 1 L; bunkhouse 38 D +
1 E + 1 L; plaza well/stalls/lamps/firebowl/tree/benches ≈ 60 D + 9 E + 4 L;
mobius2 122 D + 1 E; lissajous 4 D + 1 E + 1 M; murals 50 D + 2 E; gate 21 D
+ 1 E; lookout 15 D + 1 E + 1 L; shrine 22 D + 1 E + 1 M; paths 25 P +
5 E; planters/barrels/banners ≈ 10 D + 6 E; interior lights 4 L; earlier
fixes ≈ 6 F.)
